NOTICE OF PUBLIC HEARING AND AGENDA
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS
Thursday, August 11, 2022 *SPECIAL MEETING
ONE OR MORE SUPERVISORS MAY ATTEND VIA VIDEO AND/OR TELEPHONICALLY 
The Navajo County Board of Supervisors will hold a special meeting open to the public on Thursday, August 11, 2022, in the
Supervisors’ Chamber in the Navajo County Governmental Complex, Holbrook, Arizona. The Board may vote to meet in a closed
executive session to discuss certain matters as indicated on the following agenda. The Board may meet in executive session
pursuant to A.R.S. § 38-431.03(A) (3) for legal advice on any item.
Conference Call number: 1-877-820-7831
Participant Passcode: 6068060 #
Persons with disabilities who need accommodation to attend or participate in the meeting may contact the Clerk of the Board’s office
at (928) 524-4053; at least 48 hours prior to the meeting, so an accommodation can be arranged.
Items on the agenda may be considered out of order at the Chairman’s discretion. A copy of the agenda background material provided
to the Board, with the exception of items to be considered in executive session, is available for public inspection at Navajo County
website, www.navajocountyaz.gov 
9:00 a.m. Call To Order: 
           
1. SUPERINTENDENT OF SCHOOLS:  
 
a. Consideration and approval of the submitted 2023 School District Tax Rates.  
Presented By: Jalyn Gerlich
 
2. ELECTIONS:  
 
a. Consideration and approval of the Official Canvass of the August 2, 2022, Primary Election.  
Presented By: Rayleen Richards
 
3. ADJOURN  
 
Dated: August 10, 2022 NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS 
Posted: 6:40 a.m. By: MWB                         
Melissa W. Buckley - Clerk of the Board

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisors Special 1. a.        
Meeting Date: 08/11/2022  
Title: Consideration to approve School District Tax Rates FY 2023
Submitted For: Jalyn Gerlich, Superintendent of Schools 
Submitted By: Jalyn Gerlich, Superintendent of Schools
Department: Superintendent of Schools
Presented By: Jalyn Gerlich
Motion before the Board:
Consideration and approval of the submitted 2023 School District Tax Rates.
Background:
The Board of Supervisors per statue are required to approve the School District tax rates by the
3rd Monday in August.
Recommendation: 
To approve the submitted tax rates.

Board of Supervisors Special 2. a.        
Meeting Date: 08/11/2022  
Title: Canvass of the Aug. 2, 2022 Primary Election
Submitted By: Rayleen Richards, Elections Director
Department: Elections
Presented By: Rayleen Richards
Motion before the Board:
Consideration and approval of the Official Canvass of the August 2, 2022, Primary Election.
Background:
Pursuant to A.R.S. § 16-642
 
Canvass of election; postponements
  
The governing body holding an election shall meet and canvass the election not less than
six days nor more than twenty days following the election.
A.
Recommendation: 
staff recommends
